- 2026-11-20 05:31 SMD18 Br radius check done: 20 outputs at Br 2.60 Å, CDS unchanged. DLPNO ΔG‡ 99.6 → 93.8 kJ/mol (measured 105.1), ΔrG −108.3 → −137.2 kJ/mol. Sensitivity only; production stays on standard SMD. `03_br_radius/br_radius.md`.

**2026-11-20 09:00, measured value rechecked (J. Picione, 2026-11-20).** Dhar, Hughes and Ingold, J. Chem. Soc. 1948, 2058–2065 (doi:10.1039/JR9480002058): 2-bromopropane + 1 N NaOEt in dry ethanol, 25.0 °C. Table I 10⁵k₂ = 0.295 L mol⁻¹ s⁻¹ (corrected for solvent expansion; Table VII's 10⁶k₂ = 2.88 is the uncorrected mean of one run), 80.3 % olefin (82.7, 78.0); Table II 10⁵k(E2) = 0.237 L mol⁻¹ s⁻¹, so ΔG‡(E2) = 105.1 kJ/mol (25.1 kcal/mol) at 298.15 K, 1 M (Eyring: 105.13). Caveats from the paper: first-order processes up to 2–3 %; olefin proportions within 3 %, with a loss correction for propene. Card notes (both E2 folders), the card and `docs/references.md` updated; card and summary notes use k(E2) = 2.37 × 10⁻⁶ L mol⁻¹ s⁻¹. The earlier 2.88 × 10⁻⁶ (2026-11-09 to 11-13) is SI corrections entry 10. PDF not added to the repository.
